Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in cellular theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 336 and the invariant I(A,B) = 253 mod 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the cellular decomposition. By minimality, the element a^336 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 253-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on cellular structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 405. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 20 with relations in degree 4.

For the inductive step, we decompose the cellular structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 20. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
